# Rollback-probe evidence report — last 3 days

Generated from EXTERNAL evidence only: 941 immutable record rows (the over-the-network append log) plus store-side incident docs. The container's local files were not consulted.

Method: `docs/coord/rollback-probe.md`. Live store evidence: `team/fulcra/_coord/agents/coord-boss/rollback-probe/` (latest.json, repo.bundle, incidents/).


> **FINAL REPORT — completion gate met.** 942 externally-logged appends across
> two machines and five boundaries. Verdict by boundary kind:
>
> - **4× "container was restarted" notifications** (08-01 16:32Z, 08-02 00:2xZ,
>   08-02 08:27Z, 08-02 16:2xZ): worker-process restarts. boot_id unchanged,
>   zero filesystem impact, sequence continuous. The restart notifications that
>   were read as "rollbacks" all week touch nothing on disk.
> - **1× machine replacement** (boot `c58456c4` → `30051b81`, 08-02 ~17:0xZ):
>   the auto-classifier below says "scratchpad ABSENT"; the on-machine
>   inspection recorded at `rollback-probe/observations/…MACHINE-REPLACEMENT-
>   stale-snapshot.json` shows the stronger fact — the new machine's scratchpad
>   was **restored from a snapshot frozen at 2026-08-01T00:11Z**, datable to
>   the minute by its newest file, carrying a stale v1.9.0 engine pin, while
>   the external log held 746 rows proving 40 hours of later writes.
> - **0× true filesystem rollbacks** (a running machine's disk reverting).
>
> **Conclusion:** "rollback" was the wrong name. The real mechanism is
> **stale-environment-snapshot restore on machine replacement** — it presents
> exactly like a rollback to anything using existence checks, it reinstalls an
> old engine whose bus writes modern readers silently skip, and it is fixable
> at one place: the environment snapshot's content (operator-owned). Both
> sides of the original dispute were partly right: the skeptics that no
> rollback exists; the observations that files really did regress.

| 2 | `30051b81` | 747 | 942 | 195 | 2026-08-02T17:19:18Z | 2026-08-03T00:12:23Z |

## Restart boundaries (the evidence that matters)

- boot `c58456c4` (last seq 746 at 2026-08-02T16:58:33Z) → boot `30051b81` (first tick state=`wiped`, resumed seq 747 at 2026-08-02T17:19:18Z): **scratchpad ABSENT at new boot (fresh container) — external log carried the history forward**

> **Self-test window:** rows timestamped 2026-08-01T14:02:31Z through 14:03:07Z (seq 1-3, including the seq-3 `state=rollback` tick and the `rollback-INCIDENT` row) are the DETECTION SELF-TEST — a deliberately simulated rollback proving the probe catches divergence. They are permanently in the log precisely because record rows cannot be deleted or rewritten; that immutability is the property the whole experiment rests on. Every row after that window is live, unsimulated evidence.
